# Laravel Static Generator create-site import template v1
# Target page: index-raw_html.md
#
# How to use:
# 1. Open /admin/sites/create and click Import at the top of the page.
# 2. Choose this .txt file. The browser fills form fields and queues enabled operations.
# 3. Empty prompt blocks are ignored. If prompt is filled, AI rewrite is requested for that field.
# 4. [OPERATION] blocks are ignored until enabled = true.
# 5. For remove operations use action = remove_block / remove_last_list_item / remove_last_table_row.
# 6. Keep file/path/section_path/target_key/container_key ids unchanged unless you know the template changed.
# 7. In any [FIELD] block you can set send_current_value = true/false to control whether the current Value text is sent to AI with the prompt.
#
# Variables for this import:
# {site_name} is reused in SEO text fields.
{site_name} = Cleopatra Slot in Canada - 150 Free Spins, Free Demo & Casino Review
# {site} is reused in domain/canonical and prompts.
{site} = cleopatraslot.ca
{html_lang} = en-CA
{og_locale_alternate} = en_US
# Required. Used for canonical and generated alternate links.
{canonical_url} = https://{site}/
# Repeat {alternate_lang} as many times as needed. Alternate links are generated only when two or more languages are declared.
{alternate_lang} = en
{alternate_lang} = es
{alternate_lang} = ru
{alternate_lang} = cn
{alternate_lang} = de
{alternate_lang} = ro
{page_description} = Claim 150 Free Spins 👑 & up to 640 CAD bonus on Cleopatra slot! Free demo, no download needed, instant play for Canadian players. Start now!
# {payments__table_min_deposit_withdrawal} Переменная минимального депозита в таблице оплаты payments__table.
{payments__table_min_deposit_withdrawal} = €175/€250

Return to Player (RTP for short) is a mathematical value that indicates what proportion of bets will be repaid to the player over time.
